| l3-signed-area | summary.ideas[2] | Area counts every region as positive. It equals the integral only when $f \ge 0$. | correct | Stewart 5.2 (definite integral, properties 1-8); Apostol vol. 1, 1.24-1.27; numbers recomputed (concepts script `l3.*`) |
| l3-signed-area | summary.formula.tex | \int_a^c f(x)\,dx = \int_a^b f(x)\,dx + \int_b^c f(x)\,dx | correct | Stewart 5.2 (definite integral, properties 1-8); Apostol vol. 1, 1.24-1.27; numbers recomputed (concepts script `l3.*`) |
| l3-signed-area | summary.formula.note | Also $\int_b^a f\,dx = -\int_a^b f\,dx$ and $\int_a^a f\,dx = 0$. | correct | Stewart 5.2 (definite integral, properties 1-8); Apostol vol. 1, 1.24-1.27; numbers recomputed (concepts script `l3.*`) |
